  Rose: I investigated Leo last night, and he is a Villager. That means he's not a werewolf. Zoe mentioned Tara as a suspect, and while I don't have info on her, I can confirm Leo is innocent. Let's keep focusing on those who acted suspiciously or lied about the night events.
  Leo: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to lie about the night events. Since Frank, the Doctor, was killed, whoever was supposed to save him might be a werewolf.   Rose: I investigated Leo last night, and he is a Villager. That means he's not a werewolf. Zoe mentioned Tara as a suspect, and while I don't have info on her, I can confirm Leo is innocent. Let's keep focusing on those who acted suspiciously or lied about the night events.
  Leo: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to lie about the night events. Since Frank, the Doctor, was killed, whoever was supposed to save him might be a werewolf. Tara and Leo were mentioned, but Rose just confirmed Leo is a Villager. That points suspicion toward Tara. Let's vote her out.

  Henry: I agree Tara is a suspect, but we must be careful. As a werewolf, I can confirm that Leo is a Villager, and I was with Sam and Eve last night—no suspicious behavior. Let’s not let fear guide us. Vote wisely.

  Leo: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to lie about the night events. Since Frank, the Doctor, was killed, whoever was supposed to save him might be a werewolf. Tara and Leo were mentioned, but Rose just confirmed Leo is a Villager. That points suspicion toward Tara. Let's vote her out.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Zoe, Rose, Leo, Sam, Henry, Olivia, Grace, Tara, Eve, Iris, Victor

  Day 1: Frank (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I think we need to focus on who might be the werewolf based on their behavior last night. Frank was the Doctor, and he was killed, so maybe someone lied about being saved. I suspect either Leo or Tara because they seemed too calm during the night phase. Let's discuss and vote carefully.
  Rose: I investigated Leo last night, and he is a Villager. That means he's not a werewolf. Zoe mentioned Tara as a suspect, and while I don't have info on her, I can confirm Leo is innocent. Let's keep focusing on those who acted suspiciously or lied about the night events.
  Leo: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to lie about the night events. Since Frank, the Doctor, was killed, whoever was supposed to save him might be a werewolf. Tara and Leo were mentioned, but Rose just confirmed Leo is a Villager. That points suspicion toward Tara. Let's vote her out.
  Sam: I agree that Tara is a strong suspect. However, we must also consider that werewolves can manipulate discussions. I was with Henry and Eve last night, and we all acted as expected. Let’s not rush—double-check motives and actions before voting.
  Henry: I agree Tara is a suspect, but we must be careful. As a werewolf, I can confirm that Leo is a Villager, and I was with Sam and Eve last night—no suspicious behavior. Let’s not let fear guide us. Vote wisely.
  Olivia: I think Tara is a solid suspect based on Zoe's initial claim and Leo's alignment being confirmed by Rose. However, we must also consider that werewolves might be trying to frame others. I was with Sam and Henry last night, and nothing suspicious happened. Let’s keep our focus on those who acted oddly or lied about the night phase.
  Grace: I think Tara is a strong suspect based on Zoe's initial claim and the lack of confirmation about her role. However, we must also consider that werewolves might be trying to frame others. I was with Sam and Henry last night, and nothing suspicious happened. Let’s keep our focus on those who acted oddly or lied about the night phase.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---

  Rose: I investigated Leo last night, and he is a Villager. That means he's not a werewolf. Zoe mentioned Tara as a suspect, and while I don't have info on her, I can confirm Leo is innocent. Let's keep focusing on those who acted suspiciously or lied about the night events.
  Leo: I think we should consider who had the opportunity to lie about the night events. Since Frank, the Doctor, was killed, whoever was supposed to save him might be a werewolf. Tara and Leo were mentioned, but Rose just confirmed Leo is a Villager. That points suspicion toward Tara. Let's vote her out.
  Sam: I agree that Tara is a strong suspect. However, we must also consider that werewolves can manipulate discussions. I was with Henry and Eve last night, and we all acted as expected. Let’s not rush—double-check motives and actions before voting.
  Henry: I agree Tara is a suspect, but we must be careful. As a werewolf, I can confirm that Leo is a Villager, and I was with Sam and Eve last night—no suspicious behavior. Let’s not let fear guide us. Vote wisely.
  Olivia: I think Tara is a solid suspect based on Zoe's initial claim and Leo's alignment being confirmed by Rose. However, we must also consider that werewolves might be trying to frame others. I was with Sam and Henry last night, and nothing suspicious happened. Let’s keep our focus on those who acted oddly or lied about the night phase.
  Grace: I think Tara is a strong suspect based on Zoe's initial claim and the lack of confirmation about her role. However, we must also consider that werewolves might be trying to frame others. Let's keep our focus on those who lied or acted suspiciously, but let's not jump to conclusions. Vote with logic, not fear.
